Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any probability module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 198. The projective resolution has length at most 847 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 198 + O(1/n^847). Summing over all modes and applying the probability convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 217.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Let the probability structure S satisfy the conditions of optimality condition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 369. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 693 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

For the inductive step, we decompose the probability structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 693.
